- 管理路径沿 D17：POST /api/v1/finops/operating-expenses，POST .../{id}/approve，POST .../{id}/void，POST /api/v1/finops/rebuilds（均 Gateway→Core→FinOps）。读权限和写权限分离；FinOps 接收 Core 授权的 actor + typed scope，不接收原 mgt_。Core/FinOps 跨库无一个全局事务，Core 撤销的在途命令可能完成但必须审计 authorizedAt/commitAt。

**验收/数据契约**：两独立数据库用户互相 SQL 拒绝；Outbox 重投/Inbox ACK 丢失不双加，真实 Provider 采购原币种、员工积分、Team发行、GPU本地有据月成本不混算；FinOps down 只影响报表，Core 已知风险即时查询仍正确；历史迟到修正按原 businessDate；SQL Scope 尝试 cross-Team、分页 total、employee move 严格安全。新测试 D28-FIN01～16 与旧 FIN/FNA/MSC 全部回归。完整 V1 DDL、source-manifest 一致性算法及 SQL fixtures 尚未通过实际运行。

## 三十七、P0-D29：P12 三服务多实例、演练和量化架构验收（PROPOSED）

**业务目标**：已经运行 aigate-gateway、aigate-core、aigate-finops 三个真微服务，P12 不是“第一次把单体拆开”。应在可重建部署里证明安全故障、真实流式资源有界、2 Gateway 扩容/Redis 共享容量与 Core 强账务不变量，以及服务间独立故障隔离；性能收益如实测量，不强求线性翻倍。

**新增 Core 运维状态（先建议复用已有 Audit/Invocation，不强制新增 V12 财务表）**：Runtime operational status SERVING / ADMISSION_BLOCKED / RECOVERING / DRAINING（明确由管理员操作审计、部署配置/受信健康共同控制）；Gateway 自己 drain（拒新请求，但保留已发的处理与账务 pending），Core 的金融入场 gate 为共享权威，可有受控持久化运营状态表 runtime_admission_control(id=1,state,revision,changed_at,reason_code,actor_id) 作为 Core 后续迁移；任何一个运行实例的局部内存状态不能宣布公司 Redis/DB 故障已恢复。**这张表是否必要须用真实 P12 演练结果决定**，不为引入状态机硬建新表。

**外部运维 API（Gateway→Core，只有 PLATFORM_ADMIN，必须管理审计）**：GET /api/v1/operations/runtime-status（服务依赖 health/准入状态/时间和受信数据源）；POST /api/v1/operations/admission-block（reasonCode、Idempotency-Key，停止新收费派发、不清理已出站风险）；POST /api/v1/operations/recovery-review（referenceId、readinessEvidenceIds、decision，审批通过才允许恢复准入）；GET /api/v1/operations/reconciliation-cases?state&page&size 为受权管理员读未知账，**不存在不提供 Provider 可信证据即可一键清除 PENDING 的 API**。Gateway 实例级 DRAINING 通过可信部署管理/健康端点操作，不让单个 Core 管理命令假装能终止所有 JVM SSE。

**可重复部署/回退**：镜像 Java21/Boot4/Cloud BOM、P11 FinOps独立 V1、Core V1～V10 通过 migration job 幂等升级；production DB app 用户不可 DDL；Gateway DB 凭证恒无，FinOps不能读取 core_db。用独立 service credentials/TLS 与证书轮换，日志不含 Prompt/Response/密钥；若没有实际受信 TLS 部署则不能称“安全可生产”。任何有历史 Outbox/价格/资金的 DB 迁移必须验证向后兼容及版本N/N+1滚动发布；不可因为发布暂时忽略过期身份/策略。

**故障演练**：MySQL 失联→新收费/资金 admission fail closed；Redis 失联→所有新 Pool lease 拒绝、恢复时不能拿 ZSET 为空当真实 GPU 停止；RabbitMQ/FinOps 宕机→Ledger/Outbox 继续、只报表滞后，pending-exposure 仍由 Core 正确返回；Gateway SIGTERM/SIGKILL→拒绝新流、正在流有界 drain 或保留 pending；Provider 429/5xx→路由降级不放松安全，不盲目重发；MySQL 备份快照早于真实 Provider 出站→不可直接开放资金入场，必须与实际供应商证据审计并人工核对缺失消费。Core 若恢复了旧备份不能假装既有 Gateway/Provider 账单一律不存在。

**可量化压测协议**：同硬件、相同 MySQL/Redis/Gateway Executor 限额与 Fake Provider 模式，分别试 1/2 Gateway、JSON 0.1s/1s 与 SSE 10s、慢消费、数据授权扫描、Core 热账务行锁、FinOps 重建；记 RPS、JSON/SSE TTFT P50/P95/P99、错误率、SSE 活跃/有界缓冲、CPU、Heap/GC、JFR 线程、Hikari 活跃等待、Redis waiting/active、RabbitMQ lag、Outbox oldest/pending、pendingRisk 与采购币种。硬不变量指标：无新增未授权 Provider HTTP、无重复上游收费出站、无资金透支、无 Prompt 泄露；性能目标在有真实环境基准和团队业务SLO前不能捏造绝对 RPS/毫秒 SLA。2副本性能不必然翻倍，须解释 Core DB、Provider rate、Redis 许可硬上限可能仍是瓶颈。

**P12 与进一步微服务的 ADR 门禁**：如果真测出 Core 的治理扫描/财务热锁互相干扰，先测线程池/缓存失效/分表索引/批处理/隔离，再讨论是否有业务价值拆出第四个服务；不能为了简历把 Identity/Quota 拆出去制造分布式扣费风险。可观测性以现有 Micrometer/Actuator/JFR 和受控指标库实现，非必须先部署 Kubernetes/Nacos/全套 Service Mesh；它们是什么、解决什么、替代与代价只有明确真实运维问题才另作 ADR。

**验收**：D29-OPS01～12 与 OPS-01～24、MSC-01～24、RPC/FNA/BIZ 全量回归。要交出可复现 Compose/CI 命令、实例及数据库版本、Git SHA、实际通过/失败/跳过数、故障注入方法与安全预算 DB 断言、性能参数/图和逐服务 Runbook。**P12 完成后仍走项目 Phase Review/Code Review/测试/架构/技术债/ADR/知识/面试表达，不能自动进入“第四微服务优化”。**

## 三十八、P0-D30：Core V1 六张表完整 Flyway SQL 基准（待 MySQL 8.4 实测）

**规范优先级**：这一节是 D12+D18 的 **唯一 V1 SQL 实施基准**，代替之前散落的“五张表结构”和单独 bootstrap_control 片段。将来编码时由本地 AI 在 aigate-core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__core_identity.sql **逐字实施业务 DDL**，不要创建另外一套五表版本；如果真实 MySQL 编译失败，应把错误报告总控，由总控修改本节后重试。下面是可执行 MySQL 8.4 DDL 的**设计文本**，不是已经运行的迁移文件。所有时间由服务端应用/UTC 控制，管理命令不依赖 Redis。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE team (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  name VARCHAR(80) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_team_name (name),
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_status CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE','DISABLED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_version CHECK (version>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_name_nonempty CHECK (CHAR_LENGTH(TRIM(name))>0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE employee (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  employee_code VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  display_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  primary_team_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_employee_code (employee_code),
  KEY idx_employee_team_status_id (primary_team_id,status,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_employee_team FOREIGN KEY (primary_team_id) REFERENCES team(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_employee_status CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE','DISABLED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_employee_version CHECK (version>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_employee_display_nonempty CHECK (CHAR_LENGTH(TRIM(display_name))>0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE role_assignment (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  role_code VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  scope_type VARCHAR(16) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revoked_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_role_assignment (employee_id,role_code,scope_type,scope_id),
  KEY idx_role_scope_revoked (role_code,scope_type,scope_id,revoked_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_role_employee FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_role_kind CHECK (role_code IN ('PLATFORM_ADMIN','TEAM_MANAGER','FINOPS_READER')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_role_scope CHECK (
    (scope_type='GLOBAL' AND scope_id=0 AND role_code IN ('PLATFORM_ADMIN','FINOPS_READER'))
    OR (scope_type='TEAM' AND scope_id>0 AND role_code IN ('TEAM_MANAGER','FINOPS_READER'))
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE management_credential (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  key_prefix VARCHAR(16) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  secret_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  pepper_key_id VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  expires_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  revoked_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  issued_actor_type VARCHAR(16) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  issued_actor_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  issue_idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_management_secret_digest (secret_digest),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_management_issue (issued_actor_type,issued_actor_id,issue_idempotency_key),
  KEY idx_management_employee_active (employee_id,revoked_at,expires_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_management_employee FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_management_actor CHECK (
    (issued_actor_type='BOOTSTRAP' AND issued_actor_id=0)
    OR (issued_actor_type='EMPLOYEE' AND issued_actor_id>0)
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_management_expiry CHECK (expires_at>created_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE admin_audit_event (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  actor_type VARCHAR(16) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  actor_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  action VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  target_type VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  target_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  changed_fields_json JSON NOT NULL,
  trace_id VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  occurred_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  KEY idx_audit_time_id (occurred_at,id),
  KEY idx_audit_target (target_type,target_id,occurred_at),
  KEY idx_audit_actor (actor_type,actor_id,occurred_at),
  CONSTRAINT ck_audit_actor CHECK (
    (actor_type='BOOTSTRAP' AND actor_id=0)
    OR (actor_type='EMPLOYEE' AND actor_id>0)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE bootstrap_control (
  id TINYINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  initialized_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  initialized_by_employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bootstrap_employee FOREIGN KEY (initialized_by_employee_id)
    REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_bootstrap_id CHECK (id=1),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bootstrap_version CHECK (version>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_bootstrap_pair CHECK (
    (initialized_at IS NULL AND initialized_by_employee_id IS NULL)
    OR (initialized_at IS NOT NULL AND initialized_by_employee_id IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

INSERT INTO bootstrap_control
(id,initialized_at,initialized_by_employee_id,version,created_at,updated_at)
VALUES (1,NULL,NULL,0,UTC_TIMESTAMP(6),UTC_TIMESTAMP(6));
~~~

**数据库证明的事**：6 表存在，Team/name 与 Employee/code 物理唯一，管理秘钥 digest/签发请求唯一，员工 FK、RoleAssignment 范围枚举、审计安全 actor、bootstrap singleton 行 id=1 都受 DB 约束。

**数据库无法单凭 CHECK 证明的事**（必须由 Core Application 的同库事务和真实集成测试证明）：RoleAssignment 的 TEAM scope_id 指向 ACTIVE Team（多态 scopeId，无法直接复用同列 FK）；Team DISABLED 前没有 ACTIVE 员工；员工签发 mgt_ 时实际角色有效；成功管理动作同 admin_audit_event 原子；actor_id 的多态来源；使用秘密的 HMAC Pepper 独立 keyId/常量时间校验；首次管理初始化的 **SELECT bootstrap_control WHERE id=1 FOR UPDATE→创建五表数据→更新 singleton→COMMIT** 与竞争请求只成功一次。应用只存 secret_digest，绝不持久化完整 mgt_。

**P6 必须补显式安全迁移**：V1 ck_role_kind/ck_role_scope 只允许 P1 三种管理角色。P6 新增 POLICY_EDITOR、SECURITY_APPROVER 时 V6 迁移必须 DROP 并重新 ADD 相应 CHECK，RoleAssignment 老行保持不变，不能在 Java 里简单扩 enum 但数据库拒绝插入。建议 POLICY_EDITOR/SECURITY_APPROVER 初版只允许 GLOBAL(scope_id=0)，由真实权限审批流程验证双人操作，不因 GLOBAL 字样自动授 PLATFORM_ADMIN。

**Schemа 迁移身份**：Flyway V1 包含唯一这六张表。生产独立 migrator user 有 DDL；core_app 最小 DML+read，不能用 root；Gateway 无 datasource。MySQL DDL 不是一组可以依赖 @Transactional 自动回滚的业务语句；遇到 V1 部分建表失败只能通过受控重建**空数据库**或 migration repair 评审，不能在已有真实数据上自动删除表。**本 SQL 尚未在容器 MySQL 8.4 上执行，NOT VERIFIED。**

## 三十九、P0-D31：Core V3 七张表完整 Flyway SQL 基准与价格指针（待 MySQL 8.4 实测）

**唯一实施口径**：继 V2__runtime_identity.sql 后，在 aigate-core/db/migration/V3__catalog.sql 添加**七张表**，不改写 V1/V2 迁移。D20 中“已发布不可改”和“新版本发布时旧版有效期要终止”存在 UPDATE 旧不可变行的潜在矛盾；此处最终采用 **父对象的 activePriceVersionId 唯一指针 + 不可修改的历史 PUBLISHED 价行**。发布新版本只在事务中锁 Model/Deployment 父行、把新 DRAFT → PUBLISHED（effective_from 固定为此事务的当前DB时间，填写 published_at 一次，**首版不支持预约未来时刻自动生效**）、切换父指针、写 AdminAudit；**不 UPDATE 历史已发布单价/货币/时间**。因此旧 PUBLISHED 行可以继续保持 PUBLISHED，但不再是 ACTIVE_CURRENT，生效性由父指针和许可快照定，兼容旧历史审计。P3 刚创建但无已发布价的模型不得收费出站。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE provider (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  code VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  hosting_mode VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  base_url_host_policy JSON NOT NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_provider_code(code),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_mode CHECK(hosting_mode IN ('THIRD_PARTY_API','SELF_HOSTED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_status CHECK(status IN ('ACTIVE','DISABLED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE logical_model (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  model_code VARCHAR(96) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  display_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  model_family VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  immutable_model_revision VARCHAR(100) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  capability_profile_json JSON NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  active_points_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  active_routing_revision_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_model_code(model_code),
  CONSTRAINT ck_model_status CHECK(status IN ('ACTIVE','DISABLED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_model_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE model_deployment (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  logical_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  provider_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  deployment_code VARCHAR(96) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  endpoint_uri VARCHAR(2048) NOT NULL,
  remote_model_name VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  hosting_mode VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  region_code VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  capability_revision VARCHAR(96) NOT NULL,
  adapter_compatibility_json JSON NOT NULL,
  adapter_evidence_ref VARCHAR(256) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  cost_model VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  routing_priority INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
  routing_weight INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  max_concurrent INT NOT NULL,
  capacity_pool_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  active_purchase_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  active_credential_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_deployment_code(deployment_code),
  KEY idx_deployment_model_state (logical_model_id,status,id),
  KEY idx_deployment_provider (provider_id,status,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_deployment_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_deployment_provider FOREIGN KEY(provider_id) REFERENCES provider(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_deployment_host CHECK(hosting_mode IN ('THIRD_PARTY_API','SELF_HOSTED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_deployment_cost CHECK(
    (hosting_mode='THIRD_PARTY_API' AND cost_model='PER_TOKEN_API')
    OR (hosting_mode='SELF_HOSTED' AND cost_model='INTERNAL_RESOURCE')
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_deployment_state CHECK(status IN ('DRAFT','ACTIVE','DISABLED','MAINTENANCE')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_deployment_routing CHECK(routing_priority>=0 AND routing_weight BETWEEN 1 AND 100 AND max_concurrent>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_deployment_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE model_points_price_version (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  logical_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revision INT NOT NULL,
  point_per_1k_input DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  point_per_1k_output DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  effective_from DATETIME(6) NULL,
  published_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  published_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_model_price_revision(logical_model_id,revision),
  KEY idx_model_price_status(logical_model_id,status,revision),
  CONSTRAINT fk_model_price_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_model_price_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_model_price_publisher FOREIGN KEY(published_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_model_price_nonneg CHECK(point_per_1k_input>=0 AND point_per_1k_output>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_model_price_rev CHECK(revision>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_model_price_status CHECK(status IN ('DRAFT','PUBLISHED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_model_price_publish_pair CHECK(
    (status='DRAFT' AND effective_from IS NULL AND published_at IS NULL AND published_by IS NULL)
    OR (status='PUBLISHED' AND effective_from IS NOT NULL AND published_at IS NOT NULL AND published_by IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE deployment_purchase_price_version (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revision INT NOT NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  amount_per_1k_input DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
  amount_per_1k_output DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  effective_from DATETIME(6) NULL,
  published_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  published_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_purchase_price_revision(deployment_id,revision),
  KEY idx_purchase_price_state(deployment_id,status,revision),
  CONSTRAINT fk_purchase_price_deployment FOREIGN KEY(deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_purchase_price_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_purchase_price_publisher FOREIGN KEY(published_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_purchase_price_rev CHECK(revision>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_purchase_price_nonneg CHECK(amount_per_1k_input>=0 AND amount_per_1k_output>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_purchase_currency CHECK(currency REGEXP '^[A-Z]{3}$'),
  CONSTRAINT ck_purchase_price_status CHECK(status IN ('DRAFT','PUBLISHED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_purchase_price_publish_pair CHECK(
    (status='DRAFT' AND effective_from IS NULL AND published_at IS NULL AND published_by IS NULL)
    OR (status='PUBLISHED' AND effective_from IS NOT NULL AND published_at IS NOT NULL AND published_by IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE provider_credential (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  credential_kind VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  encrypted_secret VARBINARY(4096) NULL,
  iv BINARY(12) NULL,
  encryption_key_id VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NULL,
  credential_revision INT NOT NULL,
  issued_by_employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  issue_idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  issue_request_hmac BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  issue_hmac_key_id VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  rotated_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  revoked_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_deployment_credential_revision(deployment_id,credential_revision),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_provider_credential_issue(issued_by_employee_id,issue_idempotency_key),
  KEY idx_credential_deployment_status(deployment_id,status,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_credential_deployment FOREIGN KEY(deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_credential_issuer FOREIGN KEY(issued_by_employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_credential_issue_key CHECK(CHAR_LENGTH(issue_idempotency_key) BETWEEN 8 AND 128),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_credential_kind CHECK(credential_kind IN ('BEARER','API_KEY_HEADER','NONE')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_credential_status CHECK(status IN ('ACTIVE','REVOKED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_credential_revision CHECK(credential_revision>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_credential_fields CHECK(
    (credential_kind='NONE' AND encrypted_secret IS NULL AND iv IS NULL AND encryption_key_id IS NULL)
    OR (credential_kind<>'NONE' AND encrypted_secret IS NOT NULL AND iv IS NOT NULL AND encryption_key_id IS NOT NULL)
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_credential_revocation CHECK(
    (status='ACTIVE' AND revoked_at IS NULL) OR (status='REVOKED' AND revoked_at IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE agent_model_permission (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  agent_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  logical_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'GRANTED',
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  granted_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revoked_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  granted_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  revoked_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_agent_model_permission(agent_id,logical_model_id),
  KEY idx_agent_model_access(agent_id,status,logical_model_id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_agent_model_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_agent_model_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_agent_model_granter FOREIGN KEY(granted_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_agent_model_revoker FOREIGN KEY(revoked_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_agent_model_status CHECK(status IN ('GRANTED','REVOKED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_agent_model_revocation CHECK(
    (status='GRANTED' AND revoked_by IS NULL AND revoked_at IS NULL)
    OR (status='REVOKED' AND revoked_by IS NOT NULL AND revoked_at IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

ALTER TABLE logical_model
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_model_active_points_price
    FOREIGN KEY(active_points_price_version_id)
    REFERENCES model_points_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;
ALTER TABLE model_deployment
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_deployment_active_purchase_price
    FOREIGN KEY(active_purchase_price_version_id)
    REFERENCES deployment_purchase_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_deployment_active_credential
    FOREIGN KEY(active_credential_id)
    REFERENCES provider_credential(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;
~~~

**三个必须由 Core 事务保障的跨表不变量**：
1. 父指针必须指向**本 Model/Deployment 自己**的、status=PUBLISHED 的 PriceVersion 或属于自己的有效 ACTIVE ProviderCredential；数据库普通 FK 仅能证明 ID 存在，无法证明该 ID 属于当前父对象。管理修改事务对父行 SELECT FOR UPDATE，跨表逐项检查 parentId/status，再原子更新指针+ admin_audit_event。失效/禁用父对象时 P7 必须再次检查父状态；不得仅凭 pointer 非空信任真实可用。
2. Provider.hosting_mode、Deployment.hosting_mode 必须一致；THIRD_PARTY_API 必有已发布采购价及有效凭证（有特定合法 NONE 鉴权的 Provider 需白名单），SELF_HOSTED 的 purchase pointer 必须为 NULL。Provider/Model/Deployment status 都 ACTIVE 以及 P6 approved boundary 全部满足，才能进入最终路由硬过滤。URL/远端模型身份改变**不得只 PATCH 旧 ACTIVE Deployment 原地指到新模型**，应新建明确版本/部署 ID 并经过安全审批；失效旧 Deployment 不改历史 Invocation 归属。
3. ProviderCredential 使用**真实 AES-256-GCM**（12-byte CSPRNG unique nonce，同一加密主密钥下严禁重复），AAD=deploymentId+credentialRevision+credentialKind；非 NONE 加密密钥来自隔离的 process secret，DB 不存主密钥/明文 Provider Key；不能把 P2 不可逆 HMAC 当作要发送给供应商的 AES 加密替代。真实 Endpoint DNS/IP/重定向安全必须在 P7 连接时**再次**验证，P3 创建登记时的字符串检查不够。

**关于真正的不可变保护**：V3 SQL 没有 DB Trigger 强制阻止管理员/维护账号直接 UPDATE 已发布价表历史行；V3 管理 Service 只能 INSERT DRAFT→一次 PUBLISHED、保留旧版本并 CAS pointer，Prod app user 无直接任意 SQL HTTP。未来若法规要求在恶意 DBA 下也能证明行未篡改，则引入审计签名/独立归档，而非把普通应用层不可变称为密码学防篡改。**在本项目 P0 最终冻结前仍须用真实 MySQL 同时发布和错误 FK 归属测试证明上述代码事务能够拒绝越权版本。**

**将来 P8**：本 V3 预留了 logical_model.active_routing_revision_id（尚未有 FK 目标）；P8 创建 routing_policy_revision 后再 ALTER 添加 FK，**P3 不能创建指向 P8 还不存在表的 FK**。P7 capacity_pool_id 先作为正整数/NULL 配置，并在 P7 迁移中绑定真实 capacity_pool_policy FK；P3 不引用未来的容量表。V3 的纯 per-token 输入/输出价格仅支持 P7 已能证明风险上界的 Provider，额外缓存/推理计费类别未覆盖则拒绝收费出站，**不允许推断费用为零**。

**V3 SQL 为正式设计基准但尚未执行；P0 首先以真正 MySQL8.4 的 Flyway clean migrate/check FK/CHECK/联合并发为门禁。**

## 四十、P0-D32：Core V4 正常额度、外币采购与账本完整 Flyway SQL 基准（待实测）

**执行定位**：唯一文件 aigate-core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__normal_quota_billing.sql，在 V1/V2/V3 成功后迁移，**新增十张表**，不提前创建 V5 Grant、V7 Invocation，也不增加 Gateway/FinOps SQL 权限。单位 POINTS 精度 DECIMAL(20,6)、供应商原币种 DECIMAL(20,8)，ledger delta 采用 DECIMAL(20,8) 可无损容纳 Points 六位；Instant 全 UTC。前阶段已审批的商用真实账务事实只能 INSERT/追加修订，不能直接 UPDATE Ledger/Outbox payload。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE quota_policy (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  scope_type VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revision INT NOT NULL,
  weekly_limit_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  effective_from DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  effective_to DATETIME(6) NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_quota_policy_revision(scope_type,scope_id,revision),
  KEY idx_quota_policy_effective(scope_type,scope_id,status,effective_from),
  CONSTRAINT fk_quota_policy_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_policy_scope CHECK(scope_type IN ('AGENT_WEEK','EMPLOYEE_WEEK')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_policy_status CHECK(status IN ('DRAFT','ACTIVE','RETIRED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_policy_amount CHECK(weekly_limit_points>=0 AND revision>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_policy_period CHECK(effective_to IS NULL OR effective_to>effective_from)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE quota_bucket (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  bucket_kind VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  agent_scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  period_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  limit_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  used_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  policy_revision INT NOT NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_quota_owner_week(employee_id,bucket_kind,agent_scope_id,period_start),
  KEY idx_quota_employee_period(employee_id,period_end),
  CONSTRAINT fk_quota_employee FOREIGN KEY(employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_bucket_kind CHECK(bucket_kind IN ('AGENT_WEEK','EMPLOYEE_WEEK')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_scope CHECK(
    (bucket_kind='AGENT_WEEK' AND agent_scope_id>0)
    OR (bucket_kind='EMPLOYEE_WEEK' AND agent_scope_id=0)
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_period CHECK(period_end>period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_nonneg CHECK(limit_points>=0 AND used_points>=0 AND reserved_points>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_quota_within CHECK(used_points+reserved_points<=limit_points)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE provider_spend_budget (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  company_scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  currency CHAR(3) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  period_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  limit_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
  settled_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  risk_state VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_provider_budget_period(company_scope_id,currency,period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_budget_currency CHECK(currency REGEXP '^[A-Z]{3}$'),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_budget_period CHECK(period_end>period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_budget_nonneg CHECK(
    limit_amount>=0 AND settled_amount>=0 AND reserved_amount>=0
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_budget_risk_state CHECK(risk_state IN ('ACTIVE','FROZEN'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE provider_spend_partition (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  budget_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  partition_code VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
  limit_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
  settled_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_provider_partition(budget_id,partition_code),
  CONSTRAINT fk_partition_budget FOREIGN KEY(budget_id) REFERENCES provider_spend_budget(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_partition_kind CHECK(partition_code IN ('NORMAL','EMERGENCY')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_partition_nonneg CHECK(limit_amount>=0 AND settled_amount>=0 AND reserved_amount>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE finance_operation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  operation_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  agent_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  logical_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  service_class VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',
  status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  points_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  provider_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NULL,
  employee_team_snapshot_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  employee_week_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  company_period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  max_input_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  max_output_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  max_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  max_currency_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NULL,
  usage_evidence_digest BINARY(32) NULL,
  overrun_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  overrun_currency_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_finance_operation_key(operation_key),
  KEY idx_finance_employee_created(employee_id,created_at,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_employee FOREIGN KEY(employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_deployment FOREIGN KEY(deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_points_price FOREIGN KEY(points_price_version_id) REFERENCES model_points_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_purchase_price FOREIGN KEY(provider_price_version_id) REFERENCES deployment_purchase_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_finance_team_snapshot FOREIGN KEY(employee_team_snapshot_id) REFERENCES team(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_finance_status CHECK(status IN (
    'RESERVED','SETTLED','RELEASED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','OVERRUN'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_finance_class CHECK(service_class IN ('NORMAL','LOW','EMERGENCY','LOCAL_FALLBACK')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_finance_currency_pair CHECK(
    (provider_price_version_id IS NULL AND currency IS NULL AND max_currency_amount IS NULL)
    OR (provider_price_version_id IS NOT NULL AND currency IS NOT NULL AND max_currency_amount IS NOT NULL)
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_finance_max_nonneg CHECK(max_points>=0 AND
    (max_currency_amount IS NULL OR max_currency_amount>=0) AND
    overrun_points>=0 AND overrun_currency_amount>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE points_reservation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  finance_operation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  reserved_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  settled_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_points_operation(finance_operation_id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_points_reservation_operation FOREIGN KEY(finance_operation_id) REFERENCES finance_operation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_points_reserved CHECK(reserved_points>=0 AND settled_points>=0 AND settled_points<=reserved_points),
  CONSTRAINT ck_points_state CHECK(state IN ('RESERVED','SETTLED','RELEASED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','OVERRUN'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE reservation_allocation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  points_reservation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  source_type VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  source_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  priority_order INT NOT NULL,
  reserved_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  settled_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  released_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_allocation_source(points_reservation_id,source_type,source_id),
  KEY idx_allocation_source(source_type,source_id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_allocation_reservation FOREIGN KEY(points_reservation_id) REFERENCES points_reservation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_allocation_source CHECK(source_type IN (
    'AGENT_WEEK','EMPLOYEE_WEEK','GRANT','COMPANY_FALLBACK','CORPORATE_EMERGENCY'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_allocation_nonneg CHECK(
    reserved_points>=0 AND settled_points>=0 AND released_points>=0 AND
    settled_points+released_points<=reserved_points
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_allocation_priority CHECK(priority_order>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE provider_spend_reservation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  finance_operation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  budget_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  partition_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  reserved_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
  settled_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_provider_reservation_operation(finance_operation_id),
  KEY idx_provider_reservation_budget(budget_id,partition_id,state),
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_reservation_operation FOREIGN KEY(finance_operation_id) REFERENCES finance_operation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_reservation_budget FOREIGN KEY(budget_id) REFERENCES provider_spend_budget(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_reservation_partition FOREIGN KEY(partition_id) REFERENCES provider_spend_partition(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_reservation_values CHECK(reserved_amount>=0 AND settled_amount>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_provider_reservation_state CHECK(state IN (
    'RESERVED','SETTLED','RELEASED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','OVERRUN'
  ))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE ledger_entry (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  finance_operation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  event_kind VARCHAR(48) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  source_type VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  source_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  unit VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NULL,
  delta_amount DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
  business_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  source_revision BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  operation_event_key VARCHAR(192) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_ledger_business_event(operation_event_key),
  KEY idx_ledger_operation(finance_operation_id,id),
  KEY idx_ledger_account(source_type,source_id,business_at,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_ledger_finance_operation FOREIGN KEY(finance_operation_id) REFERENCES finance_operation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_ledger_unit CHECK(
    (unit='POINTS' AND currency IS NULL)
    OR (unit='CURRENCY' AND currency IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE outbox_event (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  event_id CHAR(36) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  business_event_key VARCHAR(192) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  aggregate_type VARCHAR(48) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  aggregate_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  aggregate_revision BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  event_type VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  schema_version SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  company_scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  business_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  payload_json JSON NOT NULL,
  payload_digest CHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  publish_state VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
  publish_attempts INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  next_attempt_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  owner_token VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NULL,
  lease_until DATETIME(6) NULL,
  last_error_code VARCHAR(64) NULL,
  published_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_outbox_event_id(event_id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_outbox_business_event(business_event_key),
  KEY idx_outbox_due(publish_state,next_attempt_at,id),
  KEY idx_outbox_lease(lease_until,publish_state),
  KEY idx_outbox_aggregate(aggregate_type,aggregate_id,aggregate_revision),
  CONSTRAINT ck_outbox_status CHECK(publish_state IN (
    'PENDING','CLAIMED','PUBLISHED','RETRY','QUARANTINED'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_outbox_attempts CHECK(publish_attempts>=0 AND schema_version>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_outbox_claim_pair CHECK(
    (publish_state='CLAIMED' AND owner_token IS NOT NULL AND lease_until IS NOT NULL)
    OR (publish_state<>'CLAIMED')
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
~~~

### 40.1 V4 真实业务不可只靠 CHECK 的约束

- 价格归属：points_price_version 必须属于 finance_operation.logical_model_id，purchase_price_version 必须属于选中第三方 deployment_id，其 currency 与已发布币种相同；表中的普通 FK 只验证目标存在，由 Core 同事务校验真正归属及现行发布指针。
- 员工来源：quota_bucket 的 AGENT_WEEK.sourceId>0 必须是真实已有 Agent 并属于当前 employee+agent 的业务周；GRANT、COMPANY_FALLBACK、CORPORATE_EMERGENCY 是 **V5/V9/V10 才激活的 source_type**，V4 即允许枚举存储以免 ALTER 破坏历史，但 P4 业务 Facade 严禁用不存在的未来资金源；多态 source_id 必须按类型锁真实来源行并校验 Owner。
- Provider 双限制：锁 budget 总行→partition 行，确认同 budgetId、currency 和 NORMAL class。任何普通 Reserve 都必须检查 settled+reserved+incoming<=limit 的两行条件；预算有 OVERUN 异常时 risk_state=FROZEN。**不在 budget/partition 表使用 settled+reserved<=limit 的 DB CHECK**，因为真实 Provider 已经产生的可信收费可能突破事先上界；必须忠实记账并阻止未来新准入，不能让 MySQL CHECK 拒绝真账单。用于准入的硬限额与“外部供方实际收费无法回滚”是两种不同性质的保证。
- Employee Points 正常账户仍受 DB used+reserved<=limit 约束。供应商实际超过已预留 Token 风险上界时 finance_operation.status=OVERRUN、overrun_points/overrun_currency_amount 保存已知差额、Ledger 写异常事实、相关 Company Currency budget FROZEN；**对无法合法分配到已批准积分来源的超额部分，不强行使 quota_bucket 负可用，不伪装 SETTLED**，进入人工有凭证的对账/补偿。金额错误不能通过“多给一个假的普通周桶”消除。
- P4 独立财务测试使用内网受信测试 facade，不建立真实 Invocation。只有 P7 真实 Provider + 可信 usage 才写真正 INVOCATION_USAGE_SETTLED / PROVIDER_COST_ESTIMATE_SETTLED；P5 真实 Grant 发行可从发行时写 TEAM_GRANT_ISSUED。Outbox payload 只存最小事实/冻结价引用/业务归属，不含 Prompt/原回复/API Key/完整 Provider HTTP Body。
- **锁序须在后续 D33 收敛**：不同 operationKey 同时扣相同员工桶和同币种月预算，不得出现把钱分别只扣一边的部分提交。没有 FinanceOperation 预建行时先 INSERT 唯一业务键；重复同 key 读取旧请求 digest、状态，**不同 body 重用相同键409**，不能因唯一键拒绝就“再生成一个新操作号”。
- V5 真 Grant发行将引用 ledger_entry（finance_operation_id 可空）及出站事件、V9/V10 通过 reservation_allocation.source_type 扩展，V7 Invocation 自己加 finance_operation_id UNIQUE FK，不允许对 V4 先建未来 Invocation 的反向 FK。

**重要未冻结项**：P4 的真实 SETTLE 按各种 Provider usage 类别如何计算可信最大费用、Overrun 的审批调整与对账证据、跨币种/跨价版本锁序、复用上述列的全部 DTO 是后续 P0 验证对象。SQL 为可执行形状的设计基准，**还没有实际 Flyway 迁移验证，不得宣布 V4 通过**。

## 四十一、P0-D33：跨 Phase 唯一 API/事务/错误和可靠性合同（设计冻结候选）

本节将 D16～D32 分散语义收敛为**唯一交互规范**，P0 最终审查以此为优先，旧 D03/D06/D10 中与此矛盾的字段/路径不能再并行实现；未列举的业务公开接口仍沿各 Phase 既有合同，不因本章被取消。

### 41.1 HTTP 统一 Envelope（客户与内网分开）

- 外部仅 Gateway：所有管理路径 /api/v1/** 转发 Core 并由 Core 校验 mgt_，P1 两个 /api/v1/bootstrap/** 受限 bst_ 单独认证；运行时 POST /v1/chat/completions 要 **agk_ + empk_**，绝不接受 mgt_ 代替；GET /v1/invocations/{id} 限被认证的原 Employee-Agent 调用身份；FinOps 外部 /api/v1/finops/** 也是 Gateway→Core 授权代理，FinOps 无公网入口。
- HTTP 失败统一 application/json：**{ "error": { "code": "VERSION_CONFLICT", "message": "The resource was modified", "requestId": "r-001", "retryable": false } }**。不可向用户回显 Core URL/SQL/密钥、供应商 HTTP Body、是否存在越权 Team 记录；404/403 依数据范围规则固定且在真实 E2E 对列表 total 做断言。重试标志**不等于“可以再调用 Provider”**，只有无副作用的读 API 或允许的完全相同业务结果报告可以安全重试。
- Core P1/P2 管理成功 DTO 按 D12/D19：**{items,page,size,total}**、id 数字和 decimal **字符串**（例如 "12.000000"），Instant UTC 8601 Z，ID 不接受科学计数，page默认1 size默认20最大100。未知 JSON 字段拒绝，所有 Controller Bean Validation 之前仍由 Core 做真正权限检验；Role scope 原始使用者只能看到允许 Team、不能将别 Team 数据先分页后内存过滤。
- 对所有会创建 Key/费用/授权/资金/出站机会的 mutation 强制 Idempotency-Key（8～128字符非空，Runtime 16～128 字符受限字符集）；幂等实际作用域默认以 actorType+actorId+operationType+rawKey 形成受控业务幂等键，但**真实物理唯一作用域以各迁移已声明为准**：P1 management_credential 是 issued_actor_type+issued_actor_id+issue_idempotency_key；P2 运行 Key 在各自表中是 issued_by_employee_id+issue_idempotency_key；P4 finance_operation.operation_key 全局唯一；普通安全 mutation 如需复合业务范围必须在相应 Phase 的显式 receipt 表持久化。任一真实物理粒度下同一键+不同 canonical request digest 一律409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT；签发明文 Key 只可首次返回，之后同键返回409 KEY_ALREADY_ISSUED_NOT_REVEALABLE；**有其他非敏感操作结果可重取的，可返回先前相同安全结果**，但永远不能再次生成费用或出站 attempt。
- 工程统一资源错误：400 VALIDATION_ERROR/INVALID_SCOPE；401 UNAUTHENTICATED/INTERNAL_UNAUTHENTICATED；403 FORBIDDEN/GOVERNANCE_DENIED；404 RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND；409 VERSION_CONFLICT/INVALID_STATE/IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT；402 NORMAL_POINTS_INSUFFICIENT/CORPORATE_POOL_INSUFFICIENT；422 BILLING_BOUND_UNVERIFIABLE/UNSUPPORTED_PROTOCOL/BUFFERED_RESPONSE_REQUIRED；429 ADMISSION_QUEUE_FULL/RATE_LIMITED；503 CORE_UNAVAILABLE/DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE/ANALYTICS_UNAVAILABLE/ADMISSION_BLOCKED；504 CORE_RESPONSE_TIMEOUT/UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT（上游超时**仍可能计费**）。其中 P1～P3 不要求实现尚未引入业务阶段的错误码；只需共同错误 JSON Shape。
- 不使用客户端 self-reported modelDeploymentId、fundingSource、dataClass 降级、serviceClass、actorEmployeeId、providerUrl 或 teamId 承担未授权的真实身份；允许客户端上报更严格 dataClass，但不得降低 Core 可信等级。

### 41.2 六类 Gateway→Core 受信 RPC 的精确前置与后置不变量

| 内部路径 | 必须验证与最小参数 | 成功后允许 | 网络失联/重复语义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST /internal/v1/invocations/preflight | svc_gateway_core + 原 agk_/empk_，idempotencyKey/requestDigest、LogicalModel、stream、受控消息/参数；Core P2/P3双ACL+P6策略快照并登记 Invocation | 仅可进 Redis WAITING，不占财务、不发 Provider | ACK 丢失可安全查询 Invocation ID，但不能按新 ID 创建两笔 |
| POST /internal/v1/invocations/{id}/dispatch-permits | svc_gateway_core + 真实双身份+Core 验证 Redis lease owner/token/pool 及绝对 deadline，最终 Deployment/Policy/Price/风险上界/真实原始消息 | 在**一笔 Core MySQL 事务**里建唯一 finance_operation+两本预留+不可变 DispatchPermit；只有本次**首次明确成功响应**且 Gateway 尚持对应 lease、自己尚未发送才可发送一次上游 | ACK 丢失后即使查询 COMMITTED 也**禁止**重复取得可发送许可；返回只读状态供保守对账 |
| GET /internal/v1/invocations/{id}/dispatch-state | svc_+请求自身关联、Invocation ID | 只查看{executionState,billingState,permitState,attemptConsumed?}最小元数据 | 永不返可重新发 Provider 的 attemptToken/Key；GET 不能创建许可 |
| GET /internal/v1/invocations/{id}/provider-credential | svc_，请求在有效首次成功 Permit/唯一目标Deployment内 | Core 通过受信内部 TLS 临时返回该部署鉴权 metadata/短时内存使用原 Key；不返回给用户或日志 | 若 Gateway 已发送后掉线不能再以此接口重试全套Provider调用；旧Key吊销时必须按 Core安全规则拒绝新出站 |
| POST /internal/v1/invocations/{id}/result | svc_+关联 attemptToken，reportOperationId、可信usage/证据digest/上游状态分类 | Core 相同事务 commit Settlement/Ledger/Outbox，或记录 PENDING/OVERRUN | ACK 丢失允许**相同 reportOperationId、相同 digest 仅重报账务结果**；不同 digest409，绝不再次发 Provider HTTP |
| GET /internal/v1/invocations/{id}/view | svc_+真实 agk_/empk_重新认证+Owner关系 | 返回最小Invocation视图，execution/billing分别显示 | 已完成但账务待对账不能错误标费用0 |

**最小 runtime success 示例**（只用于示意真正两轴，不代表每个 Provider 一定拥有可信 usage）：
~~~json
{
  "invocationId": "inv_8801",
  "executionState": "OUTPUT_COMPLETED",
  "billingState": "SETTLED",
  "requestedModel": "TEXT-Q4-STABLE",
  "actualModel": "TEXT-Q4-STABLE",
  "serviceClass": "NORMAL",
  "usedPoints": "12.000000",
  "supplierCost": {"currency":"USD","amount":"0.01500000"},
  "dispatchedAt": "2026-10-22T09:02:01Z"
}
~~~
当上游实际收费未知则 billingState=PENDING_RECONCILIATION、supplierCost=null，**null=未知，不是 0**。流式 SSE 只能在已通过 Core 真实准入后提交 HTTP200；此后失败事件不可变 HTTP 状态码，只可在安全 SSE error/关闭连接和 GET Invocation 视图表达。

### 41.3 权威数据所有权、事务线性化与一致锁序

**跨服务原则**：Gateway 不拿任何 Core DB 连接；FinOps 只能查询 finops_db；Core 金额、Grant、治理/价表、唯一 DispatchPermit 必须在 core_db 的同一个短事务内闭合，没有 MySQL+Redis+Provider HTTP 的“三方分布式事务”。Redis ACTIVE 是**另一个**短期容量事实、Core 必须自行受信核验，绝不完全相信 Gateway 传的 leaseToken；真正网络可能已发送的状态保守等待证据。

**所有 Core 写操作共同锁序建议**（最终仍需真 InnoDB 死锁注入演练）：
1. Core 接入层预检 JSON、做非锁定版本读取和**不上游网络**的最坏费用计算；
2. 获取/插入专用且唯一的 operation/invocation 业务键，对已存在行做锁定重读及 requestDigest 校验（无记录不能假设 SELECT FOR UPDATE 空行成功锁住）；
3. 按对象 ID 有序验证/锁 CURRENT 管理状态 Employee、Agent、EmployeeAgent/AgentModel 等身份/授权行与 Policy pointer、Catalog Model/Deployment/Price 指针；发布/撤销动作也应以同一序列锁相交的身份/政策或通过 revision version barrier 确保发布与派发最终准入有可解释的先后，不在持锁时运行长文本扫描、网络或Redis命令；
4. 采购账先锁 ProviderSpendBudget(总月/currency)→ProviderSpendPartition(NORMAL/EMERGENCY)，按 (currency,budgetId) 固定排序；财务来源锁 CorporateFallbackPool/CorporateEmergencyPool→EmployeeFallbackWeeklyCap/本地周Token cap→QuotaBucket（按PK）→TeamGrant（按 expiresAt,id）→EmergencyAuthorization/taskcap；
5. 保存/更新 PointsReservation、ReservationAllocation、ProviderSpendReservation、FinanceOperation、Ledger/Outbox、P7 Invocation/DispatchPermit，业务提交后才可以使用首次明确成功的 Permit 触发任何 Provider HTTP。财务 settlement/release/reconcile 也按**相同共享账户顺序**锁；TeamPool发放只碰自己的Pool/Grant，不得先锁 Grant 后倒序锁共享财政账户。
6. 若更新时发现版本不匹配、政策或权限已被撤销、授权时窗到期、最新部署无正确价/Secret、余额/容量 lease 不合法，必须整个事务ROLLBACK，Provider HTTP发送数=0。InnoDB死锁可在**尚未出站**的本地事务内有界随机退避重试，不能重试已可能发出 Provider 的整个逻辑 Invocation。

> **注意：上述锁序是目标标准，而不是已由数据库实测证实无环**。P0最终冻结应生成锁冲突图，针对“禁用Agent vs派发”“政策发布vs派发”“跨多币种预算更新vs结算”“Grant发行/过期vs结算”运行死锁与超时固件。一个 Spring @Transactional 不包含 Gateway/Redis/RabbitMQ 外部调用。

### 41.4 不可推断为0的三类未知

- Core 资金风险：Provider可能已收费但无可靠 usage，应保持 Reservation/PENDING_RECONCILIATION，即使 Redis lease 到期、SSE断开或 Gateway 重启；余额和供应商采购风险在 Core 实时查。
- FinOps 消息缺口：lastProcessedAt 新不等于无漏消息。**仅凭 outbox_event.id 高水位也不能证明提交完整**：MySQL 自增 ID 分配与事务提交先后可能颠倒。P11 的 source-manifest 核对必须针对一个 Core 一致性快照或持久化的有界事件清单（明确定义已提交集合/上界时间与分页），与 Inbox 的 eventId+digest 对比，再标 coverage asOf。若没有可复现无缺口证明，readModelStatus 只能 UNKNOWN/DELAYED，不能把“eventId连续”当严格无缺口依据。
- SelfHosted 月成本：没有真实租赁/能源账单及两管理员有效审批，FinOps 的 SELF_HOSTED_OPERATING_COST 不能使用“按照GPU运行小时×猜测价格”自动生真实财务事实。可另展示清晰标为估算的容量指标，不混入四口径核实支出。

### 41.5 跨 Phase 迁移的唯一版本命名

| Phase | 所属 DB | Migration | 本轮对应实施合同 |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | core_db | V1__core_identity.sql | D30 六表SQL + Bootstrap singleton |
| P2 | core_db | V2__runtime_identity.sql | D19 已提供四表完整SQL |
| P3 | core_db | V3__catalog.sql | D31 七表SQL、历史价+current指针 |
| P4 | core_db | V4__normal_quota_billing.sql | D32 十表SQL，Financial operation先于Invocation |
| P5 | core_db | V5__team_grant.sql | D22 三表+GRANT Source；DDL待转换 |
| P6 | core_db | V6__governance.sql | D23 七类治理表+显式扩 role_assignment CHECK |
| P7 | core_db | V7__invocation_runtime.sql | D24 五表+FK→V4 finance_operation |
| P8 | core_db | V8__routing_health.sql | D25 两表+实际路由/模型指针FK |
| P9 | core_db | V9__fallback_quota.sql | D26 五表+公司补足来源 |
| P10 | core_db | V10__emergency_authorization.sql | D27 五表+Active Task Slot |
| P11 | **finops_db** | **V1__finops.sql** | D28 七表，完全独立Flyway history |
| P12 | 仅实际出现新的数据要求才新迁移 | 无预先虚构的 V12 | 运维代码/观测与真实事故证据 |

**数据库 DDL 与服务器之间的兼容门禁**：历史 Flyway 已发布脚本的 checksum 不得因后续 Phase 直接改旧 V1～V4，必须新版本 ALTER；设计期可以修订未执行的计划稿，P1 真实迁移提交后即冻结原 SQL。P5～P10/FinOps 仍没有完整可执行 SQL 以及逐端点完整 JSON fixtures；仅 D30～D32 和 D19 V2 已做到 SQL 文本层。整个项目此时仍处于 P0 设计，不能把现有章节标为“所有 Phase 已可按字节编码”。

## 四十二、P0-D34：Core V5 TeamPool/Grant 三表完整 Flyway SQL（待验证）

**唯一实施文件**：V5__team_grant.sql，仅在 V4 成功后应用。D22/P5 发行金额与员工使用是两类不同账务事实，原 grant 不因到期/禁用/Employee调 Team 而改变 issuerTeamId/issuedPoints。全部金额 POINTS DECIMAL(20,6)，UTC 时间 DATETIME(6)，数据全保留，不 ON DELETE CASCADE。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE team_pool (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  team_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  period_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  limit_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  issued_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_issue_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_team_pool_period(team_id,period_start),
  KEY idx_team_pool_team_status(team_id,status,period_end),
  CONSTRAINT fk_team_pool_team FOREIGN KEY(team_id) REFERENCES team(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_team_pool_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_pool_period CHECK(period_end>period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_pool_state CHECK(status IN ('ACTIVE','FROZEN')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_pool_points CHECK(
    limit_points>=0 AND issued_points>=0 AND reserved_issue_points>=0
    AND issued_points+reserved_issue_points<=limit_points
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_team_pool_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE team_grant (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  issuer_team_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  recipient_employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  team_pool_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  grant_operation_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  issued_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  used_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  expires_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  issued_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  issued_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_grant_operation(grant_operation_key),
  KEY idx_grant_recipient_fefo(recipient_employee_id,status,expires_at,id),
  KEY idx_grant_issuer(issuer_team_id,issued_at,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_grant_issuer_team FOREIGN KEY(issuer_team_id) REFERENCES team(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_grant_recipient FOREIGN KEY(recipient_employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_grant_pool FOREIGN KEY(team_pool_id) REFERENCES team_pool(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_grant_issuer FOREIGN KEY(issued_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_grant_time CHECK(expires_at>issued_at),
  CONSTRAINT ck_grant_state CHECK(status IN ('ACTIVE','EXPIRED','REVOKED_FOR_NEW_USE')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_grant_amount CHECK(
    issued_points>0 AND used_points>=0 AND reserved_points>=0
    AND used_points+reserved_points<=issued_points
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_grant_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE team_grant_issue_receipt (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  issue_operation_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  team_pool_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  team_grant_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  actor_employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  recipient_employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  amount_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  operation_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_issue_receipt_key(issue_operation_key),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_issue_receipt_grant(team_grant_id),
  KEY idx_issue_actor(actor_employee_id,created_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_issue_pool FOREIGN KEY(team_pool_id) REFERENCES team_pool(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_issue_grant FOREIGN KEY(team_grant_id) REFERENCES team_grant(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_issue_actor FOREIGN KEY(actor_employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_issue_recipient FOREIGN KEY(recipient_employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_issue_positive CHECK(amount_points>0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
~~~

**账务事务唯一顺序**：先真实 TeamManager scope/employee/Team ACTIVE 核验→锁 team_pool 单行（余额与期间）→用真实 actor+Idempotency-Key/operation_digest 竞争唯一 receipt operationKey→创建 team_grant 并一次增加 pool.issued_points→创建 receipt/真实发行 Ledger（finance_operation_id NULL）/TEAM_GRANT_ISSUED Outbox/管理审计→COMMIT。**receipt.grantId 必须来自同事务已 INSERT 的真实 Grant**；重复 ACK/请求相同键409或回安全收据，不能多发一份。消费只锁 Grant 的 reserved/used，不动 TeamPool.issued。TeamGrant 的 issuer_team_id 必须在发行事务验证等于 TeamPool.team_id；普通 FK 各自存在不保证二者相等。到期时仅禁止新 reserve，旧已预留可以 settle；过期剩余绝不返原 TeamPool。

**Grant 决议与期限防竞态**：对员工资金来源先计算 FEFO 快照，再按锁定规则重新验证 expiresAt>dispatchTime 与状态。多个 Grant 先按 expires_at,id 选择，真正行锁需与其它财务事务统一按 ID 获取、防交叉死锁（不能一个事务 FEFO 锁序，另一个按 id 锁序）。若锁定期间到期/授额撤销则重新计算或整体拒绝，不可用过期余额。业务时间/发行人历史固定，不使用“当前 Recipient Team”替代 issuerTeam。

**设计状态**：3表 SQL 结构可供 Flyway 真验证，未创建迁移文件，NOT RUN；V6 之后如引入 Grant 修正审核，不得原地减少真实已发行 total。

## 四十三、P0-D35：Core V6 七类治理表与 Role CHECK 完整 SQL 基准（未实测）

**迁移**：V6__governance.sql。V1 role_assignment 目前只能容纳三种旧角色，P6 必须先显式扩展 CHECK，后建七类治理表。Policy pointer 采用后加 FK，不能在创建 Policy 时引用尚不存在的 Revision。全部业务表属 Core，无 Prompt 原文/原始命中密钥。

~~~sql
ALTER TABLE role_assignment
  DROP CHECK ck_role_kind,
  DROP CHECK ck_role_scope;

ALTER TABLE role_assignment
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_role_kind CHECK(role_code IN (
    'PLATFORM_ADMIN','TEAM_MANAGER','FINOPS_READER',
    'POLICY_EDITOR','SECURITY_APPROVER'
  )),
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_role_scope CHECK(
    (scope_type='GLOBAL' AND scope_id=0 AND role_code IN (
      'PLATFORM_ADMIN','FINOPS_READER','POLICY_EDITOR','SECURITY_APPROVER'))
    OR (scope_type='TEAM' AND scope_id>0 AND role_code IN (
      'TEAM_MANAGER','FINOPS_READER'))
  );

CREATE TABLE governance_policy (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  company_scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  active_revision_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,

  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_gov_company(company_scope_id),
  CONSTRAINT ck_gov_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE governance_policy_revision (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  policy_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revision_no INT NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  base_revision_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  config_json JSON NOT NULL,
  content_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  approved_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  published_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  approved_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  published_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_gov_revision(policy_id,revision_no),
  KEY idx_gov_rev_state(policy_id,status,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_gov_revision_policy FOREIGN KEY(policy_id) REFERENCES governance_policy(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_gov_revision_base FOREIGN KEY(base_revision_id) REFERENCES governance_policy_revision(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_gov_revision_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_gov_revision_approver FOREIGN KEY(approved_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_gov_revision_publisher FOREIGN KEY(published_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_gov_revision_no CHECK(revision_no>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_gov_revision_state CHECK(status IN (
    'DRAFT','UNDER_REVIEW','APPROVED','PUBLISHED','SUPERSEDED','REJECTED'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_gov_two_person CHECK(approved_by IS NULL OR approved_by<>created_by)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE governance_rule (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  revision_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,

  UNIQUE KEY uk_rule_revision_code(revision_id,rule_code),
  KEY idx_rule_order(revision_id,rule_order,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_rule_revision FOREIGN KEY(revision_id) REFERENCES governance_policy_revision(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_rule_kind CHECK(rule_kind IN ('CLASSIFY','SECRET_HARD_DENY','EGRESS_DENY','OUTPUT_REVIEW')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_rule_class CHECK(detected_class IN ('PUBLIC','INTERNAL','CONFIDENTIAL','RESTRICTED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_rule_severity CHECK(severity IN ('INFO','WARN','DENY')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_rule_order CHECK(rule_order>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE agent_egress_approval (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  agent_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  provider_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  region_code VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  revision INT NOT NULL,
  data_class_max VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
  valid_from DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  valid_until DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
  approved_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,

  UNIQUE KEY uk_egress_revision(agent_id,provider_id,region_code,revision),

  CONSTRAINT fk_egress_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_egress_provider FOREIGN KEY(provider_id) REFERENCES provider(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE deployment_data_boundary (

  CONSTRAINT ck_boundary_host CHECK(hosting_mode IN ('THIRD_PARTY_API','SELF_HOSTED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_boundary_state CHECK(security_approval_status IN ('PENDING','APPROVED','REVOKED','EXPIRED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_boundary_version CHECK(version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE governance_exception_request (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,

  CONSTRAINT fk_exception_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_exception_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,

CREATE TABLE governance_decision_audit (

  agent_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,

  deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  data_class VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,

  KEY idx_gov_decision_actor(employee_id,agent_id,created_at),

  CONSTRAINT fk_decision_employee FOREIGN KEY(employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_decision_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_decision_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_decision_deployment FOREIGN KEY(deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_decision_phase CHECK(decision_phase IN ('PREQUEUE','PREDISPATCH','OUTPUT_REVIEW') AND decision_ordinal>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_decision_class CHECK(data_class IN ('PUBLIC','INTERNAL','CONFIDENTIAL','RESTRICTED')),

ALTER TABLE governance_policy
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_governance_active_revision
    FOREIGN KEY(active_revision_id) REFERENCES governance_policy_revision(id)
    ON DELETE RESTRICT;
~~~

**应用层必须补的检查**：active_revision_id 必须确实属于同一 company policy 且已批准 PUBLISHED，数据库普通 FK 只能确保 id 存在；批准人不得为作者，政策发布锁当前指针并与最终 Permit 串行检查。已发布规则正文不能经 Core 管理 API UPDATE；可修改只有 DRAFT，新版本必须 append。AgentEgress 许可不能提升真实 Policy/DeploymentBoundary 的安全分类，RESTRICTED/硬凭证拒绝一切出站；CONFIDENTIAL 只走经审批 SELF_HOSTED 的非SSE全文输出审查。治理审计只存带保密服务 HMAC 的内容指纹和规则代码，不记 Prompt/Key/匹配片段。

**阶段外键**：governance_decision_audit.invocation_id 在 V6 只存可空数字；到 V7 才在已有真实 Invocation 表上 ALTER 加 FK，绝不引用不存在的表。P6新增角色需要 D19/P1原管理身份校验实时验证，RoleAssignment旧角色仍可读取。

**状态**：V6 SQL设计完整，未在MySQL8.4执行（NOT RUN）；必须验证 ALTER CHECK、循环 FK、策略发布并发与费用许可锁序，不能让本地AI用代码补过SQL失败。

## 四十四、P0-D36：Core V7 首次真实 Invocation/Permit 五表完整 Flyway SQL（待 MySQL 8.4 实测）

**唯一迁移文件**：V7__invocation_runtime.sql。P4 finance_operation 已存在，不允许另建一份金融账本，真实 invocation.finance_operation_id UNIQUE 反向指向 P4 操作。P6 治理审计的 invocation_id 只能在本迁移中补 FK。Provider Attempt Token 是一次性高熵保密凭证：Core 在 DB 留**摘要**，只在首次成功许可响应中临时交给受信 Gateway，不永久存明文。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE capacity_pool_policy (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  pool_code VARCHAR(96) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  scope_kind VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  max_active INT NOT NULL,
  max_waiting INT NOT NULL,
  max_queue_ms BIGINT NOT NULL,
  max_run_ms BIGINT NOT NULL,
  rpm_limit INT NOT NULL,
  admission_epoch BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_capacity_pool_code(pool_code),
  CONSTRAINT ck_capacity_kind CHECK(scope_kind IN ('PROVIDER','SELF_HOSTED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_capacity_status CHECK(status IN ('ACTIVE','DISABLED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_capacity_limits CHECK(max_active>0 AND max_waiting>=0 AND
    max_queue_ms>0 AND max_run_ms>0 AND rpm_limit>0 AND admission_epoch>=0 AND version>=0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE invocation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  public_invocation_id VARCHAR(64) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  finance_operation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  agent_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  employee_team_snapshot_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  canonical_schema_version SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  payload_hmac_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  payload_hmac_key_id VARCHAR(32) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  requested_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  final_deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  service_class VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',
  execution_state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  billing_state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NONE',
  queue_entered_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  queue_deadline_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  dispatched_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  company_time_zone_snapshot VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  policy_revision_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  points_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  purchase_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  capacity_pool_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  initial_deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  routing_revision BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  routing_reselect_count INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  route_reason VARCHAR(64) NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_invocation_public(public_invocation_id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_invocation_finance_operation(finance_operation_id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_invocation_idempotency(agent_id,employee_id,idempotency_key),
  KEY idx_invocation_employee_time(employee_id,created_at,id),
  KEY idx_invocation_billing(billing_state,created_at,id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_finance FOREIGN KEY(finance_operation_id) REFERENCES finance_operation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_employee FOREIGN KEY(employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_team FOREIGN KEY(employee_team_snapshot_id) REFERENCES team(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_model FOREIGN KEY(requested_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_deployment FOREIGN KEY(final_deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_initial_deployment FOREIGN KEY(initial_deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_policy FOREIGN KEY(policy_revision_id) REFERENCES governance_policy_revision(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_points_price FOREIGN KEY(points_price_version_id) REFERENCES model_points_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_purchase_price FOREIGN KEY(purchase_price_version_id) REFERENCES deployment_purchase_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_invocation_capacity FOREIGN KEY(capacity_pool_id) REFERENCES capacity_pool_policy(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_invocation_class CHECK(service_class IN ('NORMAL','LOW','EMERGENCY','LOCAL_FALLBACK')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_invocation_exec CHECK(execution_state IN (
    'RECEIVED','WAITING','ACTIVE','UPSTREAM_UNKNOWN','STREAMING',
    'OUTPUT_COMPLETED','FAILED','CANCELLED'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_invocation_billing CHECK(billing_state IN (
    'NONE','RESERVED','SETTLED','RELEASED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','OVERRUN'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_invocation_reselect CHECK(routing_revision>=0 AND routing_reselect_count>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_invocation_canonical_schema CHECK(canonical_schema_version BETWEEN 1 AND 3)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE dispatch_permit (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  invocation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  attempt_token_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  lease_token_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  authorized_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  permission_revision BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  policy_revision_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  points_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  purchase_price_version_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  upstream_delivery_status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNKNOWN',
  permit_state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'COMMITTED',
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_permit_invocation(invocation_id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_permit_token_digest(attempt_token_digest),
  KEY idx_permit_state(permit_state,created_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_permit_invocation FOREIGN KEY(invocation_id) REFERENCES invocation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_permit_deployment FOREIGN KEY(deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_permit_policy FOREIGN KEY(policy_revision_id) REFERENCES governance_policy_revision(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_permit_points_price FOREIGN KEY(points_price_version_id) REFERENCES model_points_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_permit_purchase_price FOREIGN KEY(purchase_price_version_id) REFERENCES deployment_purchase_price_version(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_permit_delivery CHECK(upstream_delivery_status IN ('UNKNOWN','REPORTED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_permit_state CHECK(permit_state IN (
    'COMMITTED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','SETTLED','RELEASED'
  ))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE result_report_receipt (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  invocation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  attempt_token_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  report_operation_id VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NOT NULL,
  evidence_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  evidence_type VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  processed_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  settlement_revision BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_report_operation(invocation_id,report_operation_id),
  KEY idx_report_invocation(invocation_id,processed_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_result_invocation FOREIGN KEY(invocation_id) REFERENCES invocation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_result_evidence CHECK(evidence_type IN (
    'TRUSTED_PROVIDER_USAGE','TRUSTED_LOCAL_USAGE','PROVEN_NOT_SENT','UNKNOWN'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_result_status CHECK(status IN (
    'SETTLED','RELEASED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','OVERRUN'
  ))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE reconciliation_case (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  invocation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  risk_reason VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  points_reserved DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin NULL,
  provider_risk_reserved DECIMAL(20,8) NULL,
  provider_request_id VARCHAR(128) NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN',
  evidence_ref VARCHAR(256) NULL,
  opened_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  resolved_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_reconciliation_invocation(invocation_id),
  KEY idx_reconciliation_state(status,opened_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_reconciliation_invocation FOREIGN KEY(invocation_id) REFERENCES invocation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_reconciliation_resolver FOREIGN KEY(resolved_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_reconciliation_state CHECK(status IN ('OPEN','REVIEWING','RESOLVED','ESCALATED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_reconciliation_amount CHECK(points_reserved>=0 AND
    (provider_risk_reserved IS NULL OR provider_risk_reserved>=0)),
  CONSTRAINT ck_reconciliation_currency CHECK(
    (currency IS NULL AND provider_risk_reserved IS NULL)
    OR (currency IS NOT NULL AND provider_risk_reserved IS NOT NULL)
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

ALTER TABLE governance_decision_audit
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_decision_invocation
  FOREIGN KEY(invocation_id) REFERENCES invocation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;

ALTER TABLE model_deployment
  ADD KEY idx_deployment_capacity_pool(capacity_pool_id),
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_deployment_capacity_pool
  FOREIGN KEY(capacity_pool_id) REFERENCES capacity_pool_policy(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;
~~~

### 44.1 V7 只有一个允许收费的 Permit 与两个独立状态轴

- Preflight 创建 RECEIVED/WAITING Invocation，finance_operation_id 为空、billingState=NONE；这个阶段没有真实库存资金/供应商采购风险。Gateway Redis WAITING 只保存 ID/Pool/时限，不保存完整原文；Gateway内存丢失原文就不能自动“捞队列重跑”。
- 获得真实 Redis ACTIVE 的 Gateway 向 Core 请求 Permit，Core **自 P7 引入自己的受控 Redis 客户端，并自己核验**实际 lease owner/token/pool/有效期和最新 Actor/两 ACL/Model/Price/Policy；Core 短 MySQL 事务内创建 FinanceOperation、预留 Points 与原币种预算、写不可重复 DispatchPermit 及绑定 Invocation.finance_operation_id、billingState=RESERVED。**先 COMMIT 后返回本次唯一明文 attempt token**，只第一次明确收到成功且lease仍有效的Gateway有机会发送上游一次；RPC ACK丢失的只读状态不可再次获得发送权。
- ResultReceipt 仅保留 attemptToken摘要，reportOperationId在一次 Invocation 内 UNIQUE。同 requestId同证据可重报同结果，不允许不同证据覆写；处理 Result report 的 Core 事务更新 Finance/Reservation/Invocation、追加Ledger/Outbox、更新Permit State及Receipt。Provider可能已出站而报告未知，reconciliation_case保留原两种预算真实敞口。不得为 UNKNOWN 出站设 Billing=RELEASED。
- 输出 JSON/SSE 的 executionState 与 billingState 严格分离。收到完整 JSON 或 SSE DONE 不保证第三方真实usage可信；UNKNOWN费用为null，不得伪造0。真实Provider连接建立、收到部分Byte之后绝不因429/5xx或客户端重连再次向第二部署发送。
- V7 Permit 唯一键证明**系统只允许一次业务 Attempt**，不能证明外部供应商天然 exactly-once；出站写入字节与 Core DB 事务物理无法原子提交，保守 UNKNOWN 是必须付出的代价。不能靠 Redis TTL 自动判断 GPU任务完结/退款。

### 44.2 与其它阶段保持一致的受限状态

- P7 的 serviceClass/Allocation 表中 LOW/EMERGENCY/LOCAL_FALLBACK 枚举只是**结构兼容位**，只能运行 NORMAL；P9 才启用LOW和单次经同意跨Model，P10才启用EMERGENCY独立批准及三档权重4:2:1。
- Core 保证一次真实资金操作可关联至多一 Invocation（UNIQUE）且一个 Invocation 唯一 Permit；P7还应校验 finance_operation.employeeId/agentId/modelId/价格版本确实与 Invocation/Permit 一致，普通 FK 找到另一用户对象也不能当成合法关系。
- Redis/Gateway 是慢I/O 和长SSE，禁止在 Core 财务 MySQL 事务中等待 HTTP Provider/扫描 Prompt/Redis Lua。Provider凭证只根据此 Permit 通过独立受控 Core→Gateway 内部 TLS 临时送Gateway有界内存，不写核心日志。
- **落地前关键技术验证**：真实 MySQL8.4 V1→V7迁移（含两个 ALTER FK）、同 Invocation 两Gateway竞争Permit、主资金事务锁死注入、ACK丢失、Provider假网络计数、Redis租约丢失、SSE断包、账务状态并行观察。全部 NOT RUN。

## 四十五、P0-D37：Core V8 Routing Health 两表完整 Flyway SQL（待实测）

V8__routing_health.sql 只补 Router 真正有必要的两个数据对象。V3 logical_model 已预留 active_routing_revision_id，**本阶段才创建目标表并添加外键**。V3 model_deployment 原本已有 routing_priority/routing_weight/max_concurrent/capacity_pool_id，不另建同义列。Redis 仍存真实短窗口和ACTIVE lease，MySQL仅存策略与低频诊断快照。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE routing_policy_revision (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  logical_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  revision INT NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  window_seconds INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
  min_samples INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
  degraded_rate DECIMAL(4,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.200,
  open_rate DECIMAL(4,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.500,
  consecutive_failure_threshold INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
  cooldown_seconds INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
  health_stale_seconds INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 120,
  probe_max_active INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  load_tie_tolerance DECIMAL(4,3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.100,
  max_pre_dispatch_reselections INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  config_digest BINARY(32) NOT NULL,
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  published_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  published_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_routing_model_revision(logical_model_id,revision),
  KEY idx_routing_model_state(logical_model_id,status,revision),
  CONSTRAINT fk_route_policy_model FOREIGN KEY(logical_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_route_policy_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_route_policy_publisher FOREIGN KEY(published_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_route_state CHECK(status IN ('DRAFT','ACTIVE','RETIRED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_route_positive CHECK(revision>0 AND window_seconds>0 AND
    min_samples>0 AND consecutive_failure_threshold>0 AND
    cooldown_seconds>0 AND health_stale_seconds>0 AND probe_max_active>0
    AND max_pre_dispatch_reselections>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_route_rate CHECK(degraded_rate BETWEEN 0 AND 1 AND
    open_rate BETWEEN 0 AND 1 AND degraded_rate<open_rate
    AND load_tie_tolerance BETWEEN 0 AND 1)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE deployment_health_observation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  deployment_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  observed_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  window_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  window_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  sample_count INT NOT NULL,
  failure_count INT NOT NULL,
  consecutive_failure_count INT NOT NULL,
  ttft_p95_ms BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  observed_state VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  source_kind VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  observation_revision BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_health_observation_revision(deployment_id,observation_revision),
  KEY idx_health_observed(deployment_id,observed_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_health_deployment FOREIGN KEY(deployment_id) REFERENCES model_deployment(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_health_window CHECK(window_end>=window_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_health_samples CHECK(sample_count>=0 AND failure_count>=0 AND
    failure_count<=sample_count AND consecutive_failure_count>=0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_health_state CHECK(observed_state IN (
    'HEALTHY','DEGRADED','UNHEALTHY','UNKNOWN','THROTTLED','CONFIG_ERROR'
  )),
  CONSTRAINT ck_health_source CHECK(source_kind IN ('PASSIVE','NONBILLABLE_ACTIVE'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

ALTER TABLE model_deployment
  ADD COLUMN health_check_mode VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DISABLED',
  ADD COLUMN approved_health_endpoint VARCHAR(2048) NULL,
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_deployment_health_mode CHECK(
    health_check_mode IN ('DISABLED','APPROVED_NONBILLABLE')
  );

ALTER TABLE logical_model
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_model_active_routing_revision
  FOREIGN KEY(active_routing_revision_id) REFERENCES routing_policy_revision(id)
  ON DELETE RESTRICT;
~~~

**真实业务版本**：管理员发布策略时 SELECT logical_model FOR UPDATE，必须检查 candidate.logical_model_id 等于本 Model、仍 DRAFT、参数合法，然后发布新 Revision 与替换唯一 active 指针/管理审计同一事务；旧策略可标 RETIRED，但已被 Invocation 冻结的 routingRevision ID 不能删除。只有 P8 确定真实 Provider 状态的权利在 Gateway 的受信观测，不允许运行时普通用户通过HTTP自己上传 HEALTHY。UNKNOWN 探路仍受所有额度/P6安全/物理容量许可。

**状态**：SQL未在 MySQL8.4运行；P8 的 Redis健康窗口和半开共享探路实际参数必须用多Gateway压测校验。

## 四十六、P0-D38：Core V9 保底资金与本地 Token 五表完整 Flyway SQL（待实测）

**唯一迁移** V9__fallback_quota.sql：不重复 P4 资金账户，增公司fallback月池与每员工周cap、可批准跨LogicalModel保底政策以及独立本地Token周用量/风险预留。P9只有在首次客户明确 fallback_mode=ALLOW_APPROVED、P6治理允许、P3目标模型真授权之后才能启用LOW。不能给公司Token补贴伪造“0美元逐次Provider付款”。

~~~sql
CREATE TABLE fallback_route_policy (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  agent_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  requested_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  fallback_model_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  fallback_kind VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
  policy_revision INT NOT NULL,
  valid_from DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  valid_until DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  created_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  approved_by BIGINT UNSIGNED NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_fallback_policy_revision(agent_id,requested_model_id,fallback_model_id,policy_revision),
  KEY idx_fallback_candidate(agent_id,requested_model_id,status,valid_until),
  CONSTRAINT fk_fallback_agent FOREIGN KEY(agent_id) REFERENCES agent(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_fallback_requested FOREIGN KEY(requested_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_fallback_model FOREIGN KEY(fallback_model_id) REFERENCES logical_model(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_fallback_creator FOREIGN KEY(created_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_fallback_approver FOREIGN KEY(approved_by) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_fallback_kind CHECK(fallback_kind IN ('PAID_API','LOCAL_BASIC')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_fallback_status CHECK(status IN ('DRAFT','APPROVED','DISABLED')),
  CONSTRAINT ck_fallback_valid CHECK(valid_until>valid_from AND policy_revision>0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_fallback_actual_change CHECK(fallback_model_id<>requested_model_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE corporate_fallback_pool (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  company_scope_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  period_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  limit_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  used_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  risk_state VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_corporate_fallback_month(company_scope_id,period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_corporate_fallback_period CHECK(period_end>period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_corporate_fallback_bal CHECK(
    limit_points>=0 AND used_points>=0 AND reserved_points>=0
    AND used_points+reserved_points<=limit_points
  ),
  CONSTRAINT ck_corporate_fallback_state CHECK(risk_state IN ('ACTIVE','FROZEN'))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE employee_fallback_weekly_cap (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  period_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  limit_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL,
  used_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_points DECIMAL(20,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_employee_fallback_week(employee_id,period_start),
  CONSTRAINT fk_fallback_cap_employee FOREIGN KEY(employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_fallback_cap_period CHECK(period_end>period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_fallback_cap_nonneg CHECK(
    limit_points>=0 AND used_points>=0 AND reserved_points>=0
    AND used_points+reserved_points<=limit_points
  )
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE local_fallback_quota (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  employee_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  period_start DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  period_end DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  limit_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  used_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reserved_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  policy_revision INT NOT NULL,
  version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_local_fallback_employee_week(employee_id,period_start),
  CONSTRAINT fk_local_fallback_employee FOREIGN KEY(employee_id) REFERENCES employee(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_local_fallback_period CHECK(period_end>period_start),
  CONSTRAINT ck_local_fallback_within CHECK(used_tokens+reserved_tokens<=limit_tokens AND policy_revision>0)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE local_fallback_reservation (
  id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  finance_operation_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  quota_id BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  reserved_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  settled_tokens BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY(id),
  UNIQUE KEY uk_local_fallback_operation(finance_operation_id),
  KEY idx_local_fallback_quota(quota_id,state),
  CONSTRAINT fk_local_reservation_finance FOREIGN KEY(finance_operation_id) REFERENCES finance_operation(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT fk_local_reservation_quota FOREIGN KEY(quota_id) REFERENCES local_fallback_quota(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  CONSTRAINT ck_local_reservation_points CHECK(settled_tokens<=reserved_tokens),
  CONSTRAINT ck_local_reservation_state CHECK(state IN (
    'RESERVED','SETTLED','RELEASED','PENDING_RECONCILIATION','OVERRUN'
  ))
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;
~~~
